### [1] [ORCA: Observability-Grounded Program Repair for Microservice Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17018)
*Yuanchen Gao,Yifang Tian,Yiran Li,Charles Zhang,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ORCA，一个基于可观测性的微服务事故自动程序修复（APR）流水线，通过配对遥测数据生成故障签名、定位候选代码与配置位置，并由修复图代理和探索代理生成补丁，最终通过遥测驱动的补丁验证器评估。在575例基准上，ORCA在成本效益上优于所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务故障通常从运维遥测中诊断，但现有APR系统依赖问题报告、代码上下文或失败测试，导致遥测诊断与补丁生成之间存在鸿沟。

Method: ORCA首先从配对（失败/正常）遥测中提取差异形成故障签名，利用签名定位候选代码与部署配置位置；随后修复图代理和探索代理从这些位置生成统一差异补丁候选；最后通过遥测驱动的补丁验证器评估补丁的合法性、语法与语义正确性、测试预言完整性和遥测重放。

Result: 在575例基准测试中，ORCA在成本效益方面优于所有评估的基线方法；结果表明运维遥测可转化为可操作的修复上下文，遥测驱动的验证能揭示仅依赖问题或测试评估会遗漏的修复结果。

Conclusion: 配对遥测数据能支撑面向修复的定位，修复图代理可将代码与配置证据转化为约束化的补丁生成上下文，遥测驱动的验证能更好地暴露真实修复效果，填补了遥测诊断与自动补丁生成之间的缺口。

Abstract: Microservice failures are often diagnosed from operational telemetry. However, automated program repair systems usually start from issue reports, localized code context, or failing tests. This mismatch leaves a gap between telemetry-based diagnosis and patch generation. We present ORCA, an observability-grounded APR pipeline for microservice incidents. ORCA first distills the differences in paired failure and reference telemetry into a fault signature, then uses the signature to identify candidate code and deployment-configuration locations. Repair graph agents and an Exploration agent generate unified-diff patch candidates from these locations. ORCA evaluates generated patches with a Telemetry-Grounded Patch Verifier that separates patch validity, syntactic and semantic correctness, test-oracle integrity, and telemetry replay. On a 575-case benchmark, ORCA outperforms all evaluated baselines in terms of cost-effectiveness. Results show that operational telemetry can be transformed from diagnostic evidence into actionable repair context: paired telemetry supports repair-oriented localization, while repair graph agents convert localized code and configuration evidence into constrained patch-generation context for the LLM. Telemetry-grounded verification then exposes repair outcomes that issue- or test-only evaluation would miss.

</details>


### [2] [LadderTeam: Dual-Agent Laddering Elicitation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17029)
*Manjushree Aithal,Alexander Kotz,James Mitchell*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LadderTeam框架，利用双智能体LLM架构自动化UX线框图访谈，通过主动访谈智能体执行三种探查策略（ACV、5-Whys、JTBD）来获取可执行的软件需求，同时后台评判智能体实时监控防话题漂移。在216次模拟访谈中达到99.1%的链收敛率和81.0%的ground-truth可执行响应匹配率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阶梯访谈技术在软件需求获取中虽然有效，但存在手动流程耗时费钱、可扩展性差以及访谈者需兼顾深度挖掘与受访者行为文化约束等局限性，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出LadderTeam框架，采用双智能体LLM架构：主动访谈智能体执行三种探查策略（ACV、5-Whys、JTBD）引导需求提取；后台评判智能体评估探针-响应对并触发实时防护措施防话题漂移。引入基于脚本化真实标注的受控模拟方法论，隔离探针质量作为唯一实验变量。

Result: 在216次访谈中，LadderTeam实现99.1%的链收敛率、81.0%的整体ground-truth可执行响应匹配率（抗拒型人格86.1%，简洁型人格75.9%），所有运行中零话题漂移。

Conclusion: LadderTeam证明LLM驱动的自动化阶梯访谈能够有效替代手动流程，具备高可靠性（高收敛率、零漂移）和可扩展性，为软件需求获取提供了开源可复现的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Eliciting detailed and actionable software requirements from end-users is a critical phase in the iterative development of a software product or application. To ensure the feedback collected is detailed and actionable, software teams can leverage the laddering interview technique. While effective for ensuring granular and actionable items from the software feedback, these interviews are subject to several limitations. They are traditionally a manual process associated with a time and financial burden, limiting scalability; interviewers must balance probing for depth while managing interviewee behavioral and cultural constraints. To address these limitations, we present \textbf{LadderTeam}, an open, reproducible framework that automates UX wireframe interviews using a dual-agent Large Language Model (LLM) architecture. An active interviewer agent executes one of three probing strategies (ACV, 5-Whys, and JTBD) to elicit actionable software requirements from usability feedback comments, while a concurrent background Judge agent evaluates probe-response pairs and triggers real-time guardrails to prevent topic drift. To rigorously evaluate LLM laddering without participant variance confounds, we introduce a controlled simulation methodology utilizing scripted ground-truth transcripts to isolate probe quality as the sole experimental variable. Across 216 interviews, \textbf{LadderTeam} achieved 99.1\% chain convergence and an 81.0\% ground-truth actionable response match (86.1\% reluctant personality, 75.9\% terse personality) with zero drift across all runs. All evaluation code, all transcripts, inputs, and a live demonstration platform will be open-sourced upon acceptance.

</details>


### [3] [A Multi-Surface Consistency Audit of Software Citation Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17159)
*Pengyin Shan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对117个开源研究软件项目进行了元数据表面（citation文件、DOI记录、包注册表等）的一致性审计，发现83.9%的项目在核心字段上存在冲突，且一半冲突源于表面描述的是论文而非软件本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究软件项目在多个地方自我描述（如CITATION.cff、DOI注册记录、包注册表等），但这些"元数据表面"之间的一致性从未被直接量化测量过。不同索引服务和自动化代理可能读取不同的表面子集，表面间的不一致会悄然割裂学术信用和溯源信息。

Method: 审计了117个开源研究软件项目（87个高性能计算/量子计算项目+30个来自JOSS和pyOpenSci的已接受包作为注册基线），每个项目最多比较7个机器可读的元数据表面。采用四级判定标准，覆盖6个元数据字段，在338行分层样本上验证了98.5%的手工判定精度。开发并发布了可导入的审计流水线库，包含语料库、采样协议、原始快照和完整验证日志。

Result: 在62个暴露至少两个可比表面的项目中，52个（83.9%）存在至少一个核心字段冲突，该结果对模糊匹配阈值不敏感。一半的跨表面冲突源于单一机制：表面描述的是软件的论文而非软件本身。32个在CITATION.cff中指定了首选引用的项目中，28个路由到了与软件自身元数据不一致的记录。作者列表和标题冲突最严重，注册表表面最不一致。

Conclusion: 研究软件项目的自我描述表面之间存在普遍且显著的不一致性，严重影响学术信用归属和溯源可靠性。需要关注元数据表面定义的一致性，特别是避免将论文元数据与软件元数据混淆。

Abstract: Research software projects describe themselves in many places at once: citation files in the repository, archive deposits, DOI registry records, package registries, and README text. We treat the software as the underlying object and these machine-readable self-descriptions as its surfaces: the points where people and automated systems read what the project declares about the software. Citation guidance, indexing services, and automated agents may read a different subset of these surfaces, so disagreement between them can silently fragment credit and provenance. This paper asks a simple question that has not been measured directly: when a project's own metadata surfaces are compared with each other, how often do they agree? We audited 117 open-source research software projects, comprising an 87-project high-performance computing and quantum computing corpus and a 30-project registered baseline drawn from the JOSS and pyOpenSci accepted-package lists, across up to seven machine-readable surfaces per project. Using a four-level verdict rubric across six metadata fields, with 98.5\% hand-verified verdict precision on a 338-row stratified sample, we found that 52 of the 62 projects exposing at least two comparable surfaces (83.9\%) contain at least one core-field conflict, a result that is insensitive to the fuzzy-matching threshold. Half of hand-adjudicated cross-surface conflicts trace to a single mechanism: surfaces describing the software's paper rather than the software itself. Among projects whose CITATION.cff includes a preferred citation, 28 of 32 route citations to a record that disagrees with the software's own metadata. The author lists and titles disagree the most, and the registry surfaces are the least aligned. We release the audit pipeline as an importable library, the corpus, the registered sampling protocol, all raw snapshots, and the complete verification log.

</details>


### [4] [Grounding AI Agents in Contracts: An Empirical Evaluation of Spec-Driven Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17177)
*Michele Tufano,James McClure,José Cambronero,Runxiang Cheng,Sherry Y. Shi,Renyao Wei,Dorothy Chen,Franjo Ivančić,Livio Dalloro,Pat Rondon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"规范驱动的测试生成"方法，通过让LLM智能体先推理并明确记录代码的前置条件、后置条件和未定义行为，形成半形式化规范作为认知支架，再指导后续测试生成。在Google生产级缺陷上的评估显示，相比传统测试生成基线，该方法在缺陷检测率上提升了9.8个百分点，分支覆盖率提升了2.5个百分点，且生成的测试套件在最佳实践、可读性和边界用例覆盖方面均优于基线和人工编写的测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在代码任务中表现出色，但当直接提示生成测试时，往往无法充分推理代码及其底层契约，导致遗漏影响测试质量的边界情况和行为边界。

Method: 提出Spec-Driven Test Generation方法：指令智能体首先推理并显式记录代码的前置条件、后置条件和未定义行为，将这些半形式化规范作为认知脚手架，引导后续测试生成过程。

Result: 在Google生产缺陷上的评估中，规范驱动智能体相比传统基线在缺陷检测率上提升9.8个百分点（p=0.0352），分支覆盖率提升2.5个百分点（p=0.0034）。LLM-as-a-Judge评估显示，生成的测试套件在77.8%和56.7%的情况下分别优于基线和人工编写的测试，且在最佳实践、可读性和边界用例覆盖方面均有改进。

Conclusion: 规范驱动的测试生成通过引入显式的半形式化规范步骤，显著提升了LLM智能体生成测试的质量，包括更高的缺陷检测率、更好的分支覆盖率和更优秀的测试套件整体质量，为基于LLM的自动化测试生成提供了一种有效的认知引导策略。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly used for coding tasks, where they have outperformed many classical approaches and scaled to repository-level tasks, such as test generation. However, when directly prompted to generate tests, these agents can fail to reason about the code and its underlying contracts, thereby missing edge cases and behavioral boundaries that affect test quality. To address this limitation, we propose Spec-Driven Test Generation, where we instruct an agent to first reason about -- and explicitly document -- code pre-conditions, post-conditions, and undefined behaviors. This intermediate semi-formal specification acts as a cognitive scaffold to guide subsequent test generation. Our evaluation on production bugs from Google shows that the spec-driven agent can deliver a 9.8 percentage points ($p = 0.0352$) improvement in bug detection rate and a 2.5 percentage point ($p = 0.0034$) improvement in branch coverage, compared to a traditional test generation agent baseline. Using LLM-as-a-Judge, we further show that test suites generated by the spec-driven agent are superior to the baseline and human-authored tests in 77.8% and 56.7% of the cases, respectively, and demonstrated improvements on following best practices, readability, and edge-case coverage.

</details>


### [5] [Graphectory Viewer: A Tool for Process-Centric Analysis of Agentic Software Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17195)
*Charlie Jyu,Shuyang Liu,Reyhaneh Jabbarvand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了Graphectory Viewer，一个用于软件智能体轨迹交互式、过程中心分析的可视化工具，可将异构原始轨迹转化为阶段感知图，支持多框架轨迹分析、节点级检查、搜索过滤及Sankey式阶段转换摘要，并作为开源工具发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的软件智能体轨迹分析通常仅关注最终任务结果，缺乏对执行过程的深度理解工具，难以从大量轨迹中识别行为模式、比较成功与失败运行，因此需要一个能够连接低层执行细节与高层行为结构的过程中心分析工具。

Method: 基于前期Graphectory表示，将异构原始轨迹转化为阶段感知图（phase-aware graphs），连接低层执行细节与高层行为结构；支持多种智能体框架的轨迹导入、交互式图构建、节点级思维/动作/观察检查、大规模轨迹集合的搜索与过滤，以及Sankey式问题解决阶段转换摘要。

Result: 该工具支持研究者对单次执行进行细粒度检查、识别反复出现的行为模式、比较成功与失败运行，并分析超越最终任务结果的大规模轨迹语料。工具连同文档、预计算图和大规模轨迹语料库以开源形式发布。

Conclusion: Graphectory Viewer为软件智能体轨迹分析提供了一种过程中心的可视化范式，提升了轨迹分析的可解释性和可扩展性，并通过开源发布促进可复现研究与后续工作。

Abstract: We present Graphectory Viewer, a web-based tool for interactive, process-centric analysis of software-agent trajectories. Building on the Graphectory representation introduced in our previous work, Graphectory Viewer transforms heterogeneous raw trajectories into phase-aware graphs that connect low-level execution details with higher-level behavioral structures. The tool supports trajectories from multiple agent frameworks and provides interactive graph construction; node-level inspection of thoughts, actions, and observations; search and filtering over large trajectory collections; and Sankey-style summaries of problem-solving phase transitions. These capabilities enable researchers and practitioners to inspect individual executions, identify recurring behavioral patterns, compare successful and failed runs, and analyze large trajectory corpora beyond final task outcomes. To support reproducibility and further research, we release Graphectory Viewer as an open-source artifact together with documentation, precomputed graphs, and the large-scale trajectory corpus.

</details>


### [6] [Oracles That Cannot Fail: Anchoring and the Expectation That Moves With the Fault](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17214)
*Arquimedes Canedo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"测试预言锚定"（oracle anchoring）缺陷：当测试预言从被测系统中获取期望值时，故障会同时移动测量值和期望值，导致比较结果抵消、故障无法被检测。作者将该概念扩展到完整分析框架，并在一个部署的空中交通管制模拟器上通过366个突变体、12个无模型属性套件进行了实证测量，证明锚定是检测失败的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试预言如果从被测系统中获得期望值则永远不可能失败，因为故障会同时移动测量值和期望值使其相互抵消。虽然"期望值"形式在测试异味文献中已有命名，但从未在任何检索到的研究中被量化测量。作者旨在命名三类新的价值传播通道，并将谓词限制为从突变目标流出的值，从而对该缺陷进行实证测量。

Method: 作者区分了规格锚定（specification-anchored）和状态锚定（state-anchored）的期望值，将谓词限制为从突变目标流出的值。在部署的空中交通管制模拟器上，对4个模块、366个突变体、12个无模型属性套件进行测量。随后进行三次预测性干预实验：重新锚定一个"持有预言"、对健康的去抖动预言进行状态锚定、以及引入参考模型进行对比。

Result: 无模型属性套件在366个突变体中比手写测试多检测3个突变体，且每个测试效率高6至33倍。三次干预结果：重新锚定一个持有预言恢复46个中的8个；对健康去抖动预言进行状态锚定损失19个中的4个；参考模型在该群体上的击杀与规格锚定完全一致，说明风险在锚定而非模型自由度。6个消融实例中，2个以大小比较为核心的实例承载了12个恢复突变体中的11个。

Conclusion: 测试预言锚定是导致故障检测失败的核心风险因素，其危害独立于模型是否无模型。发布的测试异味规则反而会撤销作者的有效修复。按照分析所识别的缺失编写预言后，暴露了部署中未曾发现的2个缺陷。作者承认所有测量来自单一系统和单一作者，结论的外推需谨慎。

Abstract: A test oracle that obtains its expected value from the system it is judging cannot fail. If a fault moves measurement and expectation together the comparison cancels exactly, and no generated input will reveal it. The defect is in the oracle and not in the input space. We call this oracle anchoring. An expectation is specification-anchored when composed from values fixed outside the code under mutation, and state-anchored when it flows, directly or transitively, from that code. The expected-value form is named in the test-smell literature but not measured in any study we retrieved. We name three further channels by which such a value reaches a verdict, restrict the predicate to values flowing from the mutate target, and measure it. The subject is a deployed air traffic control simulator with 12 model-free property suites. Across 4 modules and 366 mutants these add 3 mutants of detection over the hand-written tests, while remaining 6 to 33 times as efficient per test. We then intervene three times, predicting each outcome first. Re-anchoring one holding oracle on published procedure, changing no production code, recovers 8 of 46; state-anchoring a healthy debounce oracle costs 4 of 19; and a reference model on that population kills exactly what specification anchoring kills, placing the risk in anchoring and not in model-freedom. Of 6 instances ablated, the two sizing their comparison carry 11 of the 12 recovered mutants. The published smell rule would revert our repair. Writing the oracle this analysis said was missing then exposed two defects deployment had not surfaced. All measurements come from one system by one author.

</details>


### [7] [SNIPTEST: Fuzzing Multi-Level Code Slices for Validating Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17396)
*Aniruddhan Murali,Nobble Saji Mathews,Mahmoud Alfadel,Meng Xu,Meiyappan Nagappan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SNIPTEST是一个基于执行的警告分类框架，通过生成并模糊测试围绕静态分析警告的编译代码切片来验证漏洞，采用逐层扩展上下文的切片策略，在97个真实漏洞和97个误报的基准测试中分别正确分类了54.6%和55.6%的案例，并成功发现了CVE-2025-11964。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析工具产生的警告需要大量人工检查来确认是否为真实漏洞，而将定向模糊测试应用于整个项目来验证每个警告在计算上不可行，通常需要数天执行时间且覆盖率提升有限。

Method: SNIPTEST采用执行基础的警告分类框架，围绕静态分析警告生成并模糊测试编译代码切片。它使用逐层切片策略，围绕目标位置增量扩展上下文，以提高精度验证潜在漏洞，而非在完整程序中证明可利用性。

Result: 在97个真实漏洞和97个误报的基准上，SNIPTEST对54.6%的确认漏洞产生"可能的真实阳性"证据，对55.6%的确认误报产生"可能的假阳性"证据；在40.2%的漏洞案例中，其利用路径与前三个栈帧匹配；成功识别了CVE-2025-11964。

Conclusion: SNIPTEST通过代码切片和渐进式上下文扩展，为静态分析警告提供了一种计算上可行的执行验证方法，能够在不过度消耗计算资源的情况下有效分类漏洞和误报，并在实际场景中发现了真实的安全漏洞。

Abstract: Modern software systems are increasingly complex, and static analysis tools are commonly used to identify potentially vulnerable code by issuing warnings. However, these warnings often require manual inspection to confirm whether the reported issues are real, making the process time-consuming and error-prone. Directed fuzzing has emerged as a powerful automated technique to validate the warnings. However, applying it to the entire project in response to each warning is computationally infeasible, often requiring days of execution to achieve only incremental improvements in code coverage.
  We present SNIPTEST, an execution-based warning triage framework that generates and fuzzes compiled code slices centered around static-analysis warnings. Rather than proving exploitability in the full program, SNIPTEST provides evidence about how a warning behaves under progressively expanded sliced execution contexts. It employs a layer-by-layer slicing strategy, incrementally expanding context around the target location to validate potential vulnerabilities with increasing precision. We evaluate SNIPTEST on a benchmark of 97 true vulnerabilities and 97 false alarms across three real-world projects. SNIPTEST produces Possible True Positive evidence for 53 of 97 confirmed vulnerabilities (54.6%) by triggering the corresponding bug oracle consistently across all three analyzed slice levels, while the remaining cases are unreachable. Particularly, in 40.2% of these cases, it exploits the vulnerability along the observed execution path, matching the top three stack frames. On the 97 confirmed false alarms, SNIPTEST produces Possible False Positive evidence for 54 cases (55.6%) by reaching the warning without triggering the bug oracle, but misclassifies 28 cases (28.8%),and the remaining cases are unreached. Finally, we demonstrate the practical relevance of SNIPTEST by identifying CVE-2025-11964.

</details>


### [8] [COMMITGUARD: Differential Slice Fuzzing for Commit-Induced Bug Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17401)
*Aniruddhan Murali,Noble Saji Mathews,Mahmoud Alfadel,Meiyappan Nagappan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出COMMITGUARD，一种基于提交感知的差分切片模糊测试方法，用于验证代码变更。它通过对比提交前后函数的可编译代码切片，仅报告提交后版本新出现的bug，在300个真实提交上将518个sanitizer报告缩减到7个候选bug，其中5个被确认为真实bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件通过频繁提交进行演化，但代码审查和测试对内存安全问题提供的保障有限。对每个提交进行全程序模糊测试不切实际，因为成本高、需要合适的harness，且可能无法覆盖到提交改动的代码路径，因此需要一种高效且针对性强的方法来验证提交引入的bug。

Method: COMMITGUARD是一种提交感知的差分切片模糊测试方法。核心思想是利用提交前版本的修改函数作为行为基线来解读提交后发现的bug。对每个目标提交，它识别修改的函数，从提交前后两个版本提取可编译的代码切片，独立对成对的切片进行模糊测试，然后比较两个版本的sanitizer报告，仅将提交后版本新出现的bug报告为候选的提交引入bug。

Result: 在来自openSSL、libpcap和leptonica的300个提交上评估，切片模糊测试初步产生518个sanitizer报告，通过前后切片对比缩减到7个候选提交引入bug报告。人工验证确认5个为真实bug（已被项目开发者修复），仅2个为误报。平均分析一个提交耗时32.4分钟，修改函数平均覆盖率达到75.36%。

Conclusion: COMMITGUARD通过差分切片模糊测试有效地将大量模糊测试报告缩减为少量需要人工审查的候选提交引入bug，在保证高效率（平均32.4分钟/提交）和较高覆盖率（75.36%）的同时，显著降低了人工分类的成本。

Abstract: Modern software systems evolve through frequent commits that implement bug fixes, features, and security patches. Although code review and testing are widely used to check these changes, they often provide limited assurance for memory-safety issues. Code reviewers may miss subtle boundary, lifetime, or initialization errors, while existing tests may not exercise the specific paths affected by a commit. Fuzzing is effective at exposing such bugs, but applying it to every commit remains impractical because whole-program fuzzing is expensive, requires suitable harnesses, and may still fail to reach the code changed by a commit.
  In this paper, we introduce COMMITGUARD, a commit-aware differential slice-based fuzzing approach for verifying code changes. The key insight behind COMMITGUARD is that the pre-commit version of a modified function can serve as a behavioral baseline for interpreting bugs found after the commit. For each target commit, COMMITGUARD identifies modified functions, extracts compilable code slices from both the pre-commit and post-commit versions, and fuzzes the paired slices independently. It then compares sanitizer reports across the two versions and reports bugs that emerge only in the post-commit version as candidate commit-induced bugs. We evaluate COMMITGUARD on 300 commits from openSSL, libpcap and leptonica. Slice fuzzing initially produces 518 sanitizer reports across these commits. By comparing pre-commit and post-commit slices, COMMITGUARD narrows this large output to 7 candidate commit-induced bug reports that require manual triage. Manual validation confirms 5 of these reports as real bugs that were fixed by developers of the examined projects after we reported them, while only 2 reports were classified as false positives. COMMITGUARD analyzes a commit in 32.4 minutes on average and achieves 75.36% average coverage of modified functions.

</details>


### [9] [Beyond FLOPs: Energy-Aware Knowledge Distillation for Sustainable LLMs on Code-Related Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17515)
*Enrique Barba Roque,Luís Cruz,Annibale Panichella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了能源感知的知识蒸馏方法在软件工程任务中的应用，通过对比FLOPs和能量替代模型作为优化指导指标的可靠性，发现直接使用能量替代模型能更好地降低LLM的推理能耗（最高降低90%）和内存使用（降低86%），同时仅带来有限的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程任务中表现出色，但其高计算需求和能源消耗带来可持续性挑战，限制了在消费硬件和资源受限平台上的部署。现有文献常用FLOPs作为计算成本的报告指标，但其是否能准确反映实际能耗尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 采用受控实验，使用基于多目标优化的蒸馏方法Morph，在克隆检测和漏洞预测任务上检验FLOPs能否准确反映能耗；扩展该方法引入直接估计CPU和GPU能耗的能量替代模型；并利用CodeT5+将Morph应用于代码摘要等生成任务。

Result: FLOPs并非始终是能耗的可靠指标，使用能量替代模型可以获得更好的优化效果。蒸馏后的学生模型推理能耗最高降低90%，内存占用减少86%，仅伴随适度的精度权衡。

Conclusion: 在能量感知知识蒸馏中，使用直接能量替代模型而非FLOPs作为指导，可以有效改善LLM在软件工程应用中的能耗、可持续性和可部署性，使高效模型能够在消费级硬件上运行。

Abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being applied to Software Engineering (SE) tasks, achieving high accuracy across problems such as clone detection, vulnerability prediction, and code summarization. However, their high computational demands and energy consumption raise sustainability concerns and hinder their use on consumer hardware and resource-constrained platforms. A common way to report the computational cost of an LLM in the literature and industry is to use the number of Floating Point Operations (FLOPs) required to perform a pass over the network. Aims: This paper investigates the implications of energy-aware knowledge distillation for SE, aiming to improve model efficiency while maintaining performance and to determine whether FLOPs is a reliable energy-aware metric. Method: We conduct a controlled experiment using Morph, a Many-Objective Optimization-based distillation methodology, to empirically examine whether FLOPs accurately reflect energy consumption in Clone Detection and Vulnerability Prediction tasks. We extend this methodology to include energy-surrogate models that directly estimate CPU and GPU energy consumption during optimization, and we apply Morph to generative tasks using CodeT5+ for code summarization. Results: Our results show that FLOPs is not always a reliable indicator of energy consumption, and better results can be achieved by using energy-surrogate models. Distilled student models can reduce inference energy consumption by up to 90\% and memory usage by 86\%, with only modest accuracy trade-offs. Conclusions: Energy-aware knowledge distillation when guided by direct energy surrogates rather than FLOPs can improve the energy consumption, sustainability, and deployability of LLMs for SE applications, enabling efficient models on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [10] [REST API Testing with Verified LLM-Inferred Dependencies and Response-Driven Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17546)
*Tu Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen,Huy Nguyen,Viet Nguyen,Tien N. Nguyen,Vu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: APIPilot是一个面向REST API测试的执行验证框架，通过将LLM推断的依赖关系视为假设并通过实际API执行进行验证，再基于验证后的依赖图构建覆盖感知的工作流，显著提升了操作覆盖率、代码覆盖率和执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的REST API测试方法直接将LLM推断的依赖关系视为正确，缺乏基于执行结果的验证，容易引入虚假依赖、遗漏可行的操作链并产生不可行的测试用例。

Method: APIPilot首先结合结构启发式规则和LLM语义推理，从OpenAPI规范中推导候选的生产者-消费者依赖关系；然后将这些依赖关系视为假设，通过具体的API执行进行验证；验证后的依赖组织成语义依赖图，并通过有界top-k图遍历构建覆盖感知的工作流；最后通过响应驱动的细化机制，利用运行时响应更新资源池、调整输入生成约束，并修剪或修正无效的依赖映射。

Result: 在16个真实世界REST API服务上的实验表明，APIPilot实现了92.3%的操作覆盖率、最高58.6%的代码覆盖率以及88.1%的工作流执行成功率，优于基于LLM和传统的REST API测试基线。此外还检测到197个独特的5xx失败和规范-执行不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 将依赖推断扎根于执行反馈可以显著提升REST API测试的有效性，APIPilot验证了"推断-验证-构建"这一范式在REST API测试中的有效性。

Abstract: Testing RESTful APIs requires generating sequences of API calls that satisfy dependencies among operations, parameters, and runtime-created resources. Recent LLM-based approaches infer such dependencies and generate test sequences from OpenAPI specifications, but they often treat LLM-inferred relationships as correct without execution-based validation. This can introduce spurious dependencies, miss feasible operation chains, and produce infeasible tests. In this paper, we propose APIPilot}, an execution-validated framework for REST API testing. APIPilot first derives candidate producer-consumer dependencies from OpenAPI specifications using structural heuristics and LLM-based semantic reasoning. It then treats these dependencies as hypotheses and validates them through concrete API executions before using them for test generation. The validated dependencies are organized into a dependency graph from which APIPilot constructs coverage-aware workflows via bounded top-k graph traversal, separating semantic dependency inference from sequence construction. To improve subsequent tests, APIPilot further performs response-driven refinement: runtime responses are analyzed to update resource pools, adjust input-generation constraints, and prune or revise invalid dependency mappings. Empirical evaluation on 16 real-world REST API services shows that APIPilot achieves 92.3% operation coverage, up to 58.6% code coverage, and an 88.1% workflow execution success rate, outperforming both LLM-based and traditional REST API testing baselines. APIPilot also detects 197 unique 5xx failures and specification-execution mismatches, demonstrating the benefit of grounding dependency inference in execution feedback.

</details>


### [11] [Unified Message Model for Heterogeneous Serial Data Exchange Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17642)
*Viktor Sinitsyn,Florian Holzapfel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的、与协议无关的串行消息模型，用于显式和确定性地描述串行通信消息，并通过工业工具环境中的实现验证了其在自动化工具链中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代嵌入式系统日趋复杂，集成了大量异构设备，涉及广泛的串行通信协议（从标准化到完全项目自定义）。高效开发日益依赖自动化工具链，而这些工具链需要一个清晰、统一且机器可处理的正式基础。

Method: 提出一种协议无关的统一消息模型，基于数据类型、原子消息元素（容器）和完整消息结构的正式定义。同时引入实用方法，包括可配置消息类型（表达结构约束、支持确定性自动化）和可配置用户表示（面向工程的读写）。

Result: 通过工业工具环境实现验证，结果表明该方法能够支持机器可读的接口控制文档开发、传输层软件的自动生成，以及标准化和弱形式化串行协议的实际工程工作。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型、方法和工具实现为异构串行通信开发中的自动化工具链提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Modern embedded systems are becoming increasingly complex and typically integrate numerous heterogeneous devices, such as controllers, sensors, actuators, and supporting subsystems. As a result, their development and integration involve a wide variety of serial communication protocols, ranging from standardized solutions to partially standardized and fully project-defined formats. Efficient development of such systems increasingly depends on automation toolchains, which in turn require a clear, unified, and machine-processable formal basis. This paper proposes a unified, protocol-agnostic message model for explicit and deterministic description of serial messages. The model is based on formal definition of data types, atomic message elements (containers), and complete message structure. In addition to the model itself, the paper introduces methods for practical work with it, including configurable message types for expressing structural constraints and supporting deterministic automation, as well as configurable user representations for engineering-oriented reading and editing. The proposed model and methods are demonstrated through implementation in an industrial tool environment. The results show that the approach can support machine-readable interface control document development, automated generation of transport-layer software, and practical engineering work with both standardized and weakly formalized serial protocols. Taken together, the proposed model, methods, and tool implementation provide a practical foundation for automation toolchains in heterogeneous serial communication development.

</details>


### [12] [Benchmarking Automated Security Patch Backporting: How Far Are We?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17671)
*Jincheng Yang,Yulong Fu,Chengwei Liu,Lyuye Zhang,Fangyuan Zhang,Bingyang Ren,Yang Liu,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了Porting Benchmark，一个包含1,234个安全补丁回溯案例的基准数据集，覆盖跨版本、跨分支和跨仓库场景。在统一评估框架下，五种工具的表现与以往报告存在显著差异：复杂补丁上最佳提交级成功率从85.2%骤降至24.0%，并识别出四类根本原因。动态验证进一步揭示静态基准分数无法完全反映真实修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化安全补丁回溯工具在各自数据集上报告超过80%的成功率，但评估通常局限于同质化环境（单一仓库或特定版本），无法反映工具在跨版本、跨分支、跨仓库等真实场景中的泛化能力。

Method: 构建包含1,234个安全补丁回溯案例的Porting Benchmark数据集（覆盖跨版本、跨分支、跨仓库场景），设计统一的评估框架，对五类工具（涵盖程序分析、LLM提示和LLM代理）进行对齐评估，并结合45个动态验证子集（含测试用例和POC）进行可执行验证。

Result: 对齐评估改变了性能格局：PortGPT和TSBPort在Replication Dataset上保持相对强劲，而FixMorph和Mystique在统一协议下显著退化。复杂补丁上的最佳提交级成功率从Type-I的85.2%降至Type-IV的24.0%。识别出四类根本原因并验证了参考基准分数与真实修复效果之间的差距。

Conclusion: 现有补丁回溯工具在统一、多样化的评估下泛化能力有限，参考基准的静态匹配评分不足以反映真实修复质量，可执行反馈精化对最困难的案例仅提供有限改善。未来工具设计需要针对四类根本原因进行针对性改进。

Abstract: Automated security patch backporting is critical for mitigating N-day vulnerabilities. Recent tools report success rates above 80% on their respective datasets. However, these evaluations are often confined to homogeneous environments, such as one repository or specific project versions. Consequently, it remains unclear how well these tools generalize beyond their originally targeted scenarios. We present Porting Benchmark, a curated dataset of 1,234 security patch backporting cases spanning cross-version, cross-branch, and cross-repository scenarios, paired with a common evaluation framework. Using this benchmark, we evaluate five tools spanning program analysis, LLM prompting, and LLM agents under aligned settings. Our results show that aligned evaluation changes the apparent performance landscape: PortGPT and TSBPort remain comparatively strong on the Replication Dataset, while FixMorph and Mystique degrade substantially under the common protocol. Performance degrades sharply on structurally complex patches: the best commit-level success rate falls from 85.2% on Type-I patches to 24.0% on Type-IV. We identify four root-cause categories (missing target API awareness, cross-version semantic mismatch, non-local dependency propagation failure, and patch construction or localization failure) and derive concrete directions for next-generation tool design. On a 45-case dynamically validated subset with verified test cases and constructed POCs, we further observe that reference-based benchmark scores do not fully capture real-world remediation: exact match sharply under-credits harder target adaptations, while executable validation reveals residual integration failures in the target that static reference agreement misses. Executable-feedback refinement provides limited but measurable recovery on the hardest executable cases.

</details>


### [13] [GADR: Gathering Architecture Decision Records from Meeting Transcriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17694)
*Lucas Daniel Costa da Silva,Kiev Gama*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GADR，一个多智能体自纠正工作流，用于从原始会议转录文本中提取架构决策并生成Nygard格式的ADR草稿。可行性研究（5个真实会议转录、4位资深架构师评审、15名学生评估）表明该工作流能捕获大部分专家识别的决策，生成清晰实用的草稿，在稳定性和结构遵循度上优于零样本和少样本基线。同时揭示了RAG增强在提升ADR深度的同时可能引入与转录不符内容的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的ADR生成方法存在一个关键且未被充分检验的假设：输入已具备合理结构。实际上架构决策往往来自非正式、嘈杂的会议，决策是隐式的、碎片化的，并与离题对话混杂，单次提示（single-pass prompting）在这种条件下性能严重下降。

Method: 提出GADR，一个多智能体自纠正工作流，从原始会议转录中提取架构决策并生成Nygard格式的ADR草稿。通过5个真实项目会议转录开展可行性研究，由4位资深架构师进行专家评审，并由15名学生进行评估。

Result: GADR能捕获大多数专家识别的决策，生成的草稿被认为清晰且有用，在稳定性和结构遵循度方面优于零样本和少样本基线。研究还发现RAG基于检索的增强在提升ADR内容深度的同时，存在产生与转录内容不符信息的风险。

Conclusion: 多智能体自纠正工作流在从非结构化会议转录生成ADR方面具有可行性，但自动化架构文档中的可追溯性问题值得社区关注，特别是RAG增强在深度与忠实度之间的权衡提出了有待探索的开放问题。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based approaches to Architecture Decision Record (ADR) generation share a critical and largely unexamined assumption: that input is already reasonably structured. In practice, architectural decisions emerge from informal, noisy meetings where choices are implicit, fragmented, and entangled with off-topic dialogue, precisely the conditions under which single-pass prompting degrades. This paper presents GADR, a multi-agent, self-correcting workflow that extracts architectural decisions from raw meeting transcriptions and generates Nygard-formatted ADR drafts. A feasibility study comprising five real project meeting transcripts, expert review by four senior architects, and evaluation by fifteen students provides initial evidence that the agentic workflow captures most expert-identified decisions and produces drafts participants found clear and useful, outperforming zero-shot and few-shot baselines in stability and structural adherence. The study also addresses the underexplored trade-off of RAG-based enrichment improving ADR depth while simultaneously risking transcript-unfaithful content, raising open questions about traceability in automated architectural documentation that we believe is worth the community's attention.

</details>


### [14] [What Aggregate Scores Miss: Measuring Item-Level Regressions in Commercial LLM API Migrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17719)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型API迁移决策中，仅依赖聚合基准分数会掩盖双向的单条目级性能变化，导致误判风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业大语言模型API厂商会弃用旧版本，迫使依赖方迁移到新版本。但迁移决策常基于聚合基准分数，这些分数将异构的条目级行为压缩为单一净值，掩盖了重要的细节信息。

Method: 在GPT-5.4到GPT-5.6 Sol产品的三次成对升级中，对900个公共基准条目（研究生水平知识、奥林匹克数学、指令遵循）每个条目每模型查询50次，在错误发现率控制和实际显著性阈值下，将每个条目分类为可靠改善、可靠退化、实际等价或不确定，并使用标签置换零模型进行校准。

Result: 所有九个迁移基准单元中，可靠改善与可靠退化并存。聚合增益高达7.3个百分点的边界包含多达8.3%的可靠退化条目；聚合损失的边界包含多达10.7%的可靠改善条目。在指令遵循基准上，严格与宽松评分差距扩大3.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于聚合分数的迁移决策会遗漏大量双向的条目级变化，应结合条目级细粒度分析来指导模型迁移。

Abstract: Context: Software systems that depend on commercial large language model APIs must migrate to successor versions when vendors deprecate older models. Migration decisions typically rely on aggregate benchmark scores, which compress heterogeneous item-level behaviour into a single net figure. Objective: We measure what that compression conceals. Method: On three pairwise upgrades in the GPT-5.4 to GPT-5.6 Sol product sequence, we query 900 public benchmark items (graduate-level knowledge, olympiad mathematics, instruction following) 50 times per item per model, classify each item as reliably improved, reliably regressed, practically equivalent, or inconclusive under false-discovery-rate control and a practical-significance threshold, and calibrate the results against a label-permutation null. Results: Across all nine migration-benchmark cells, reliable improvements and reliable regressions coexist. Edges with aggregate gains of up to 7.3 percentage points contain up to 8.3% reliably regressed items; edges with aggregate losses contain up to 10.7% reliably improved items. On the instruction-following benchmark, the gap between strict and loose scoring widens by 3.9 percentage points on the latest migration: a 3.9-point regression under strict scoring shrinks to 0.04 points under loose scoring. Conclusion: Migration decisions based on aggregate scores alone miss substantial bidirectional item-level change. The complete response-level archive and per-item scoring outputs are released.

</details>


### [15] [SpecTrum: Specification-Guided Differential Fuzzing for Ethereum Consensus Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17738)
*Seokhun Jeong,Gyeongmin Dan,Sukyoung Ryu,Sungjae Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpecTrum是一个框架，通过机械化的Ethereum共识规范（Consensus-SpecTec）将有效性条件显式化为if-前提，定义前提覆盖率指标来评估测试覆盖度，并基于规范生成测试用例以补充未覆盖的分支。在五个主要Ethereum共识客户端上发现了27个跨客户端分歧案例，其中22个仅靠机械化规范才能发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ethereum的共识安全性依赖于多个独立共识客户端实现对每个状态转换达成一致。实现错误会导致分叉、最终性停滞和严重攻击。现有规范依赖Python参考实现，其有效性条件隐含在运行时行为中，缺乏系统化方法来确保所有有效性条件被充分测试。

Method: SpecTrum采用三阶段方法：(1) 引入Consensus-SpecTec，将Ethereum共识算法机械化，把有效性条件显式化为if-前提；(2) 定义前提覆盖率指标，衡量spectests中各if-前提被评估为真/假的程度；(3) 开发基于规范的测试生成器，从未被评估为假的约束中提取前提并生成测试输入。

Result: 在五个主要Ethereum共识客户端上应用SpecTrum，发现了27个跨客户端分歧案例，其中22个是仅添加机械化规范中的前提才能发现的。所有27个案例均能在不同分叉版本中复现。

Conclusion: SpecTrum有效解决了Ethereum规范中有效性条件隐式化的问题，能够系统性地发现跨客户端实现分歧。扩展到新的分叉版本只需与规范差异成比例的适度工作量，展现了良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Ethereum's consensus safety relies on independent consensus client implementations agreeing on every state transition. When they diverge due to implementation errors, the network can fork, finality can stall, and severe attacks are possible. To prevent such consensus divergences, Ethereum provides a Python reference implementation (consensus-spec), which acts as a specification, and a hand-crafted official test suite (spectests). However, as an executable implementation, Ethereum's specification defines validity implicitly through runtime behavior. As a result, it lacks a systematic way to ensure that all validity conditions are thoroughly evaluated.
  We present SpecTrum, a framework that addresses this problem in three stages. First, we introduce Consensus-SpecTec, a mechanized specification of the Ethereum consensus algorithm, which makes validity conditions explicit as if-premises. Second, we define premise coverage, a metric that measures which if-premises are evaluated to true and false across spectests. Third, we develop a specification-based test generator that extracts constraints on premises not evaluated to false by spectests and generates inputs to evaluate them. Applying SpecTrum to five major Ethereum consensus clients, we identify 27 cross-client divergence cases, 22 of which cannot be found without the premises inserted in our mechanization. All 27 cases reproduce across fork versions, and extending the mechanized specification to a new fork takes modest effort proportional to the specification difference.

</details>


### [16] [Reshaping the SDLC for Data- and AI-Centric Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17824)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨数据密集型与AI赋能系统如何重塑传统软件开发生命周期（SDLC），通过整合DataOps、MLOps和LLMOps实践，提出了一个包含四层贡献的框架：阶段性生命周期转型综述、轻量级形式化模型、自适应五层生命周期框架（制品、契约、门禁、控制、治理），以及概念性研究模型，指出该领域转型方向已明确但量化证据不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SDLC以源代码为中心假设系统行为主要由代码决定，但数据密集型和AI赋能系统的行为由代码、数据和模型交互涌现，且真实世界条件偏离训练数据时性能会退化，现有生命周期模型无法充分应对这些挑战。

Method: 采用跨学科文献综合方法，整合软件工程、数据管理、机器学习系统和人机交互等领域的研究；通过轻量级形式化将系统行为定义为代码、数据和模型配置的函数；构建需求评估导向的规范与统计验证门禁；提出五层生命周期框架并将维护视为配置漂移下的闭环控制问题；最后提出概念性研究模型并批判性评估证据基础。

Result: 形成了阶段化的生命周期转型叙述（覆盖需求、架构、开发、测试、部署、监控、治理和组织）；给出了行为定义与概率验收区域的形式化框架；提出了包含制品层、契约层、门禁层、控制层和治理层的五层自适应框架；建立了链接数据工程整合与生命周期结果的研究模型，并发现转型方向日益明确但其幅度量化不足。

Conclusion: 数据与AI中心系统的SDLC转型是必要且趋势明确的，但现有证据基础不足以量化其影响幅度，未来需要开展经验驱动的、自适应的SDLC研究，以建立更坚实的实证基础。

Abstract: The traditional Software Development Lifecycle (SDLC) assumes that system behavior is determined primarily by source code, allowing correctness to be specified, implemented, and verified through code-centric practices. Data-intensive and AI-enabled systems challenge this assumption because their behavior emerges from the interaction of code, data, and learned models, while performance may degrade as real-world conditions drift from training data. This paper examines how integrating data engineering and software engineering practices, operationalized through DataOps, MLOps, and LLMOps, reshapes the SDLC for these systems. We make four contributions. First, we synthesize literature across software engineering, data management, machine learning systems, and human-centered computing into a phase-structured account of lifecycle transformation spanning requirements, architecture, development, testing, deployment, monitoring, governance, and organization. Second, we provide a lightweight formalization in which system behavior is defined over code, data, and model configurations; requirements become evaluation-led specifications with probabilistic acceptance regions; and promotion is controlled through statistically grounded validation gates. Third, we develop an adaptive five-layer lifecycle framework comprising artifact, contract, gate, control, and governance layers, positioning maintenance as a closed-loop control problem under configuration drift. Fourth, we propose a conceptual research model linking data engineering integration to measurable lifecycle outcomes and critically assess the evidence base. While the direction of transformation is increasingly established, its magnitude remains insufficiently quantified. We conclude with a research agenda for an empirically grounded, adaptive SDLC for data- and AI-centric systems.

</details>


### [17] [What Does It Mean and Why Should I Bother? Motivating Students to Write Better Commit Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17993)
*Gergő Balogh,Péter Seres,László Tóth,Attila Szatmári,Szakács Bence,Ádám Zoltán Végh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法案例研究验证了软件工程教师对学生提交信息（commit message）沟通质量不佳的怀疑，基于已有质量分类法对学术与工业项目进行部分复制分析，确认了问题普遍存在。由此设计了轻量级角色扮演教育游戏WDYM，用于在课程约束下揭示和解决提交信息沟通问题。游戏在提升意识和促进反思方面有效，但未能证明对写作实践产生即时或持续改进，属于情境受限的干预工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程教师怀疑学生撰写的提交信息经常无法发挥其应有的沟通作用，但这一教学上的疑虑缺乏实证支持，因此需要系统的研究来验证并寻找应对措施。

Method: 采用本地动机的混合方法案例研究：(1)使用既有的提交信息质量分类法对学生项目与工业项目进行部分复制分析；(2)设计WDYM轻量级角色扮演教育游戏；(3)通过游戏观察与参与者调查收集数据进行效果评估。

Result: 确认沟通与质量问题在学生和工业情境中均反复出现；WDYM在提升参与者的意识并促进对提交信息沟通问题的反思方面有效，但缺乏即时或持续改进写作实践的充分证据。

Conclusion: WDYM是一个有用但不完美的、情境受限的教学干预工具，可有效应对本地观察到的教学问题，但研究不主张其普遍适用性。

Abstract: This paper reports on a locally motivated mixed-methods case study addressing a teaching-related suspicion held by software engineering instructors: that commit messages written by students frequently fail to serve their intended communicative role. To examine this suspicion empirically, we analyzed commit messages from student and industrial case-study projects using a partial replication of an established commit-message quality taxonomy. The results confirm that communication and quality issues occur recurrently in both contexts, substantiating the instructors' initial concern. Motivated by this finding, we devised What Do You Mean? (WDYM), a lightweight, role-based educational game intended to surface and address commit-message communication breakdowns within the constraints of university coursework. Analysis of gameplay observations and participant surveys shows that WDYM is effective in raising awareness and fostering reflection on commit-message communication issues, although it provides limited evidence of immediate or sustained improvement in commit-message writing practices. Taken together, the study presents WDYM as a useful, though imperfect, context-bound intervention for addressing a locally observed instructional problem, without claiming general applicability beyond the studied setting.

</details>


### [18] [The Polyglot's Dilemma: Conformance Testing a Dozen Specs in as Many Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18039)
*A. Jesse Jiryu Davis,Jeremy Mikola,Jeff Yemin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MongoDB在11年间开发了一套基于规范的测试方法，用YAML编写测试并由各语言驱动库的解释器执行，最终形成统一测试格式，删除了超过22,000行测试代码，非一致性缺陷率最多下降86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MongoDB有十余种编程语言的客户端库，每种语言都原生实现而非共享核心，且代码量庞大（数百万行）。确保这些库之间行为一致极其困难但至关重要，亟需一种高效的跨语言一致性测试方法。

Method: 采用规范驱动的测试方法：测试用例用YAML声明式编写一次，然后由每种语言特有的解释器在各库中执行。从多种临时格式逐步演进为统一的测试格式（Unified Test Format），通过模式演进来合并和标准化测试格式。

Result: 统一测试格式使得超过22,000行测试代码被删除；采用YAML测试的驱动库中非一致性缺陷率最多下降86%（但结果因库而异）。

Conclusion: 总结了在声明式测试设计、测试架构、模式演化和统一化局限性方面的经验教训，说明统一测试格式能有效提升跨语言客户端库的行为一致性。

Abstract: MongoDB maintains client libraries in a dozen programming languages, used by tens of thousands of organizations and millions of developers. Most are implemented natively rather than as wrappers around a shared core. Ensuring consistent behavior across these libraries, comprising millions of lines of code, is hard but essential. Over eleven years, we developed a specification-based testing approach: tests are written once in YAML and executed by language-specific interpreters for each library. We describe the evolution from many ad-hoc formats to a Unified Test Format, which allowed us to delete over 22,000 lines of test code. The rate of nonconformance bugs fell up to 86% in drivers that adopted YAML tests (though results varied). We report lessons learned about declarative test design, test architecture, schema evolution, and the limits of unification.

</details>

### [19] [On computational approaches to Pop music culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17812)
*Arthur Flexer*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文主张对流行音乐文化的计算研究必须超越单纯的音频分析，采用多模态方法（结合音频、视觉、文本和文化维度），借鉴数字人文中"远距离阅读"的思路，并指出了当前研究的两大问题：多模态方法稀缺和音乐语料库构建中的外部效度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行音乐文化是一个融合音频、视觉、文本和文化内涵的复杂多模态社会文化现象，但现有计算研究大多局限于音频分析，缺乏整合性的多模态研究视角。

Method: 文章采用综述和论证相结合的方法：首先论证多模态研究的必要性，其次系统回顾已有的定量分析工作，最后提出三个未来研究方向（歌词主题宇宙图谱、专辑封面图像志、音乐时间线上的复古周期追踪）。

Result: 综述发现两大突出问题：真正意义上的多模态研究方法稀缺，以及音乐语料库构建中的采样实践导致外部效度存疑。

Conclusion: 流行音乐文化的计算研究需要采用类似"远距离阅读"的整合性多模态方法，同时需解决语料库采样偏差问题，并通过歌词主题图谱、封面艺术图像志和复古周期追踪等具体路径推进未来研究。

Abstract: This overview article presents arguments why the computational study of Pop music culture needs to be conducted in a multi-modal way beyond mere audio analysis, gives a survey of already published quantitative work on analyzing Pop music at scale, and discusses challenges and promising research avenues for future work.
  We argue that Pop music culture is a rich tapestry of audio, visual, textual and cultural connotations and relations which needs to be studied in an integrative way as a multi-modal socio-cultural phenomenon. What is needed is an approach which is reminiscent of "distant reading", i.e. algorithmic analysis of thousands of books as a research tool in digital humanities. In addition to listening to audio, algorithms need to view album artwork and music videos, to read meta-information, lyrics, music magazines and books.
  Our review of already available work on distant reading/listening/viewing and multi-modal combinations thereof reveals two major open issues: a scarcity of truly multi-modal approaches and questionable external validity rooted in sampling practices when building music corpora. In trying to overcome these shortcomings we sketch three exemplary avenues for future research on Pop music culture: charting the topic universe of music lyrics, providing an iconography of album cover art, tracking retro cycles in music's timeline.

</details>

### [20] [Sparse Coverage: Semantic Center Representations for Patent Prior-Art Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16918)
*You Zuo,Kim Gerdes,Éric de la Clergerie,Benoît Sagot*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse Coverage，一种无监督语义检索框架，将局部span嵌入映射到嵌入空间中心构成的稀疏词汇表，通过覆盖导向的k-center目标选择中心点，生成兼容倒排索引检索的稀疏表示，在专利检索任务中达到或超越强稠密专利编码器的文档级召回效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利现有技术检索是面向长且高度结构化技术文档的召回导向任务。稠密检索虽能改善语义匹配，但单向量表示可能将多个技术组件、功能和约束压缩进单一嵌入中，丢失细粒度语义信息。

Method: 提出无监督的Sparse Coverage框架：1) 将局部span嵌入映射到嵌入空间中的稀疏词汇表；2) 采用覆盖导向的k-center目标函数选择中心点；3) span激活邻近中心点以产生与倒排索引检索兼容的稀疏表示。该方法结合局部语义证据与稀疏倒排索引搜索，无需监督训练。

Result: 在CLEF-IP 2013数据集上的实验表明，Sparse Coverage在多种配置下达到或超越强稠密专利编码器的文档级召回率，同时在段落级检索上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Sparse Coverage通过结合局部语义证据与稀疏倒排索引搜索，为专利检索提供了一种有效的第一阶段检索方法，兼顾语义匹配能力和倒排索引的高效检索特性。

Abstract: Patent prior-art retrieval is a recall-oriented search task over long and highly structured technical documents. Dense retrieval improves semantic matching, but single-vector representations may compress multiple technical components, functions, and constraints into a single embedding. We propose Sparse Coverage, an unsupervised semantic retrieval framework that maps local span embeddings to a sparse vocabulary of embedding-space centers. The centers are selected with a coverage-oriented k-center objective, and spans activate nearby centers to produce sparse representations compatible with inverted-index retrieval. Experiments on CLEF-IP 2013 show that Sparse Coverage matches or exceeds the document-level recall of strong dense patent encoders in several configurations, while remaining competitive for passage-level retrieval. By combining local semantic evidence with sparse inverted-index search, Sparse Coverage provides an effective first-stage retrieval approach for patent search.

</details>


### [21] [CARA: Cognitive Adaptive Recommendation Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16919)
*Weijun Gao,Jinyang Dong,Chuanru Ren,Hengxiao Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出CARA框架，将推荐建模为结构化决策过程，结合直觉情感偏好与理性评估两种机制，通过候选过滤和双视角决策建模两个阶段实现推荐，并引入边界感知KTO策略提升训练信号密度，在Amazon Reviews三个领域上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型与智能体推荐方法主要依赖语义匹配、端到端生成或松散的工作流结构，未显式建模用户偏好如何被处理并转化为最终决策，缺乏对推荐决策过程的结构化认知建模。

Method: CARA将推荐形式化为结构化决策过程，包含两个协调阶段：(1)候选过滤——基于粗粒度偏好约束缩小搜索空间；(2)双视角决策建模——分别从情感判断和理性评估两个互补视角捕捉推荐决策。此外引入边界感知KTO策略，优先训练模型偶尔能解但非一贯能解的指令，以增加信息性偏好信号的密度。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews三个领域的大量实验表明，CARA在大多数评估指标上取得最佳性能，相比基线实现了最高10.15%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模情感与理性双重决策机制，并将推荐组织为结构化决策流程，CARA能够更有效地利用大型语言模型进行上下文感知的推荐，验证了认知启发的推荐框架的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and agent-based recommendation frameworks have introduced new opportunities for more flexible and context-aware recommendation. However, existing methods still largely rely on semantic matching, end-to-end generation, or loosely structured agent workflows, without explicitly modeling how user preferences are processed and translated into final decisions. To address this limitation, we propose CARA, a cognitively inspired recommendation framework that formulates recommendation as a structured decision-making process. The core intuition of CARA is that user decisions are jointly shaped by two complementary mechanisms: intuitive affective preference and deliberate rational evaluation. Accordingly, CARA organizes recommendation into two coordinated stages: candidate filtering, which narrows the search space based on coarse-grained preference constraints, and dual-perspective decision modeling, which captures recommendation decisions through affective and rational judgment. We further introduce a boundary-aware KTO strategy that prioritizes instructions the model can solve occasionally but not consistently, thereby increasing the density of informative preference signals. Extensive experiments on three Amazon Reviews domains show that CARA achieves the best performance on most evaluation metrics, with relative improvements of up to 10.15% over the baseline.

</details>


### [22] [MITRE-SAGE: A Multi-Agent Cybersecurity Question-Answering model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16921)
*Ali Habibzadeh,Farid Feyzi,Reza Ebrahimi Atani*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MITRE-SAGE，一个多智能体检索增强生成框架，用于提升网络安全领域大语言模型问答系统的可靠性和可解释性；同时提出MITRE-QA基准（包含3,000个问答对）用于系统评估。实验表明该框架优于独立LLM和常规RAG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全运营面临信息过载、告警疲劳和时间受限决策等挑战；尽管LLM在问答任务上表现出潜力，但在网络安全领域受限于领域知识不足、幻觉问题以及难以同时捕捉语义和结构关系。

Method: MITRE-SAGE采用多智能体RAG框架，将复杂任务分解为查询理解、证据检索和答案综合三个子任务；集成语义知识与结构化网络安全知识；提出轻量级配置（Qwen2.5-7B子智能体+Qwen2.5-14B编排器）。

Result: MITRE-SAGE在8个基准任务的5个上取得最优性能，持续优于独立LLM和传统RAG方法；轻量级配置即达到优越效果。

Conclusion: MITRE-SAGE作为可扩展且可解释的方法在网络安全问答中展现出显著潜力，MITRE-QA为未来研究提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Effective cybersecurity operations require timely and accurate analysis of large-scale heterogeneous security information; however, analysts increasingly struggle with information overload, alert fatigue, and time-constrained decision-making. Although large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated promising capabilities for question answering (QA), their effectiveness in cybersecurity remains limited by insufficient domain knowledge, a tendency to hallucinate, and difficulties in capturing both semantic and structural relationships. This work proposes MITRE-SAGE, a multi-agent retrieval-augmented generation framework that integrates semantic and structural cybersecurity knowledge to improve the reliability and interpretability of LLM-based QA systems. By decomposing complex tasks into query interpretation, evidence retrieval, and answer synthesis, MITRE-SAGE effectively supports cybersecurity tasks such as vulnerability assessment, threat profiling, and relationship extraction. Furthermore, we propose MITRE-QA, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 3,000 question-answer pairs for evaluating LLMs across diverse cybersecurity knowledge tasks, and use it to systematically evaluate MITRE-SAGE against representative baseline methods. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MITRE-SAGE consistently outperforms standalone LLMs and conventional RAG approaches. Notably, a lightweight configuration comprising Qwen2.5-7B sub-agents and a Qwen2.5-14B orchestrator achieves superior performance on five of the eight benchmark tasks, indicating the effectiveness of the proposed multi-agent framework. The results highlight the potential of MITRE-SAGE as a scalable and interpretable approach for reliable cybersecurity QA, while MITRE-QA provides a standardized benchmark for future research.

</details>


### [23] [Towards welfare-oriented recommendations in activity-travel behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16922)
*Ekin Ugurel,Takahiro Yabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以用户福利为导向的活动推荐框架，通过净效用（体验收益减去旅行成本）来评估推荐选项，并设计了两个操作决策准则——正效用概率（PUP）和遗憾最小化（RM），同时开发了基于智能体的仿真环境来验证该框架在实际旅行场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流推荐系统依赖各种启发式方法进行排序，但缺乏对用户福利的原则性考量。在基于活动的出行行为中尤其严重，因为用户无论最终满意度如何都要承担无法回收的成本（如能量、时间）。现有系统可能基于流行度或协同过滤推荐选项，但仍可能让用户比邻近或自选方案更糟。

Method: 提出一个以福利为导向的活动推荐框架，以净效用（体验收益减去旅行成本）为核心评估指标。形式化两个操作决策准则：正效用概率（PUP）仅在净效用非负的概率超过阈值时推荐；遗憾最小化（RM）仅在相对于用户最佳自主选择的期望遗憾低于容忍水平时推荐。开发基于智能体的仿真，让异构合成旅行者在具有现实旅行成本、拥堵和行为反馈循环的空间环境中与多个推荐系统互动，实现受控反事实评估。

Result: 通过智能体仿真实现了对推荐准则的受控反事实评估，验证了该框架能够将用户福利作为首要目标来设计推荐系统。

Conclusion: 该框架为将用户福利作为推荐系统的主要目标而非偶然副产品提供了实用基础。

Abstract: While mainstream recommender systems (RS) rely on diverse heuristics to rank alternatives, they generally lack a principled account of user welfare (i.e., whether accepting the recommendation will leave the user better off than other alternatives). The problem is particularly acute in activity-based travel behavior, where users incur costs they cannot recoup (i.e., energy, time) regardless of eventual satisfaction. As a result, existing systems may recommend options based on popularity or collaborative filtering, but may still leave users worse off than nearby or self-selected alternatives. We address this gap by introducing a welfare-oriented framework for activity recommendation that evaluates suggestions in terms of net utility, defined as experienced benefit minus travel costs. Specifically, we formalize two operational decision criteria: Positive Utility Probability (PUP) recommends only when the probability of non-negative net utility exceeds a threshold, while Regret Minimization (RM) recommends only when expected regret relative to the user's best organic alternative falls below a tolerance level. To evaluate these criteria, we develop an agent-based simulation in which heterogeneous synthetic travelers interact with multiple RS over time in a spatial environment with realistic travel costs, congestion, and behavioral feedback loops. This framework enables controlled counterfactual evaluations, and offers a practical foundation for designing RS that treat user welfare as a primary objective rather than an incidental byproduct.

</details>


### [24] [Overview of the TREC 2025 Product Search and Recommendation Track](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17138)
*Dean E. Alvarez,Surya Kallumadi,Daniel Campos,ChengXiang Zhai,Alessandro Magnani,Rikiya Takehi,Michael D. Ekstrand*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍TREC 2025产品搜索任务（延续2023/2024），包含查询扩展和关联产品推荐两个子任务，其中关联产品推荐任务发布了区分互补品和关联品的高质量标注数据集，旨在推动电商检索和推荐系统的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商产品目录日益庞大，搜索和推荐成为电商网站的核心，但缺乏用于评估端到端检索质量的高质量数据集，阻碍了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 在TREC 2025上运行修订并延续的产品搜索评测轨道，包含两个任务：查询扩展和关联产品推荐。其中关联产品推荐任务提供了区分互补品与关联品的标注数据集。

Result: 发布了全新的关联产品推荐标注数据集，能区分互补品与关联品，为更好的推荐和搜索应用提供基础资源。

Conclusion: 该数据集有望作为对话式产品发现体验的基础构建模块，推动更符合用户需求的推荐和搜索应用发展。

Abstract: In the past few years, consumers have moved the bulk of their product exploration and purchasing efforts online seeking speed, convenience, and price comparison with ease unimaginable for in-person shopping. As product catalogs have grown in diversity and size product search and recommendation have become a cornerstone for e-commerce sites.
  Despite the widespread usage of search engines in e-commerce, there is no high-quality dataset designed to evaluate end-to-end retrieval quality. In 2025, we ran a revised and continued version of the Product Search track previously run at TREC 2023 and TREC 2024. The 2025 product search track had two tasks: query expansion and related-product recommendation. The related-product recommendation task is particularly novel, providing an annotated data set of product relationships that distinguishes between complementary and related products. We anticipate the data from this track will enable better recommendation and search applications that reflect user needs, as a building block for conversational product discovery experiences.

</details>


### [25] [Empowering Compact LLMs with Fusion of Layer-wise Exits for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17316)
*Xurong Liang,Tong Chen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Jianxin Li,Xiangliang Zhang,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出FLEXRec，一个基于判别式紧凑型大语言模型的序列推荐框架。通过在多个Transformer层插入预测头并自适应融合其分数分布，实现了高效的全文候选集排序，在Qwen 3 1.7B和Llama 3.2 3B上达到最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型推荐系统虽然性能卓越，但计算成本高昂，难以应用于实际场景。紧凑型LLM作为替代方案虽更高效，但受限于模型容量，需要依赖推理或知识蒸馏方法，这些方法会增加延迟或依赖更大的模型。此外，判别式LLM-RS虽能通过嵌入相似性实现高效全文排序，但紧凑型骨干网络在表达能力和结构适应性上存在局限。

Method: FLEXRec采用判别式框架，在多个Transformer层插入预测头（即"出口"），并自适应融合各层的分数分布。提出自适应连续路由器（AC-Router）动态选择每个用户序列的出口数量和位置，同时设计新颖的target-k hinge损失来调节路由稀疏性。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上，使用Qwen 3 1.7B和Llama 3.2 3B作为骨干网络进行实验，FLEXRec在紧凑型骨干方法中达到了最先进的准确率，同时保持了高效率。

Conclusion: FLEXRec有效解决了紧凑型LLM在推荐系统中表达能力不足和计算效率难以兼顾的问题，通过层间出口融合和自适应路由机制，在保持高效全文排序的同时显著提升了推荐准确率。

Abstract: Large language model-based recommender systems (LLM-RSs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities, but are computationally unsustainable for many real-world applications. Compact LLMs offer a practical alternative, yet their reduced capacity often requires reasoning or knowledge distillation methods that increase latency or depend on larger models. Combined with autoregressive generation, these approaches face severe scalability bottlenecks. In contrast, discriminative LLM-RSs enable efficient full-corpus ranking through embedding similarity, but compact backbones remain limited in expressiveness and structural adaptivity. We propose the Fusion of Layer-wise Exits for Sequential Recommendation (FLEXRec), a discriminative framework that enhances compact LLMs while retaining scalable full-corpus ranking. FLEXRec inserts prediction heads (i.e., exits) at multiple transformer layers and adaptively fuses their score distributions. An adaptive continuous router (AC-Router) dynamically selects both the number and identity of exits for each user sequence, while a novel target-k hinge loss regulates routing sparsity. Experiments on three real-world datasets with Qwen 3 1.7B and Llama 3.2 3B show that FLEXRec achieves state-of-the-art accuracy among compact-backbone methods while remaining highly efficient. Code: https://github.com/xurong-liang/FLEXRec

</details>


### [26] [Once Generated, Ranked: End-to-End Generative Slate Recommendation with Unified Semantic-Collaborative IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17613)
*Yang Hu,Jiayi Guo,Jingui Ma,Ning Li,Jiangling Qin,Yanming Li,Yang Deng,Xiaoshuang Chen,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出OGR框架，一种端到端的生成式榜单推荐方法，通过TUSID自适应融合语义与局部协同信息构建分层语义ID，利用列表级偏好规划和流水线位置解码直接生成有序榜单，并结合SPA策略优化方法对齐用户偏好。离线实验在工业和公开数据集上分别获得48.2%和27.2%的NDCG@5相对提升，在线A/B测试在快手平台实现1.120%的有效浏览提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有榜单推荐方法通常将候选生成与排序分离，限制了优化空间；基于语义ID的生成式推荐缺乏推荐感知的语义和局部协同信号，且next-token预测与榜单级目标不一致。

Method: 提出OGR端到端框架：(1) TUSID，自适应融合物品特定语义和局部协同信息构建分层语义ID；(2) 列表级偏好规划与流水线位置式语义ID解码，建模全局偏好和物品间依赖；(3) SPA，奖励引导的保守策略优化方法，使生成的榜单超越似然模仿对齐用户偏好。

Result: 离线实验中，OGR在工业和公开数据集上分别取得48.2%和27.2%的相对NDCG@5提升；在线A/B测试在快手平台获得1.120%的有效浏览提升。

Conclusion: OGR通过端到端生成有序榜单的方式，解决了传统榜单推荐中候选生成与排序分离以及生成式推荐中语义ID信息不足和训练目标不对齐的问题，显著提升了推荐效果。

Abstract: Slate recommendation treats a slate rather than an individual item as the recommendation unit, requiring joint optimization of item interactions and slate utility. Existing approaches typically separate candidate generation from ranking and restrict optimization to retrieved candidates. Generative recommendation with Semantic IDs (SIDs) offers a path to end-to-end recommendation, but existing SID construction often lacks recommendation-aware semantics and effective local collaborative signals, while next-token prediction is misaligned with slate-level objectives. We propose OGR, an end-to-end framework that directly generates ordered slates-"Once Generated, Ranked." OGR first introduces TUSID, which adaptively fuses item-specific semantic and local collaborative information into hierarchical SIDs. It then uses list-wise preference planning and pipelined position-wise SID decoding to model global preferences and inter-item dependencies while generating ordered slates. We further propose SPA, a reward-guided conservative policy optimization method that aligns generated slates with user preferences beyond likelihood imitation. Offline experiments show that OGR outperforms representative baselines, with 48.2% and 27.2% relative NDCG@5 gains on industrial and public datasets, respectively. Online A/B testing on Kuaishou further yields a 1.120% improvement in Effective Views.

</details>


### [27] [From Student Risk Prediction to SC2R: Semantics-Constrained Counterfactual Recourse for Educational Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17618)
*Ngoc Luyen Le,Marie-Hélène Abel,Bertrand Laforge*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SC2R框架，将语义约束的反事实追索（counterfactual recourse）应用于教育决策支持，通过结合校准预测模型、整数规划生成干预方案、RDF词汇表表示干预计划以及SHACL验证约束（时间、预算、不可变性、可用性），确保推荐不仅模型有效而且操作可行。在OULAD数据集上的离线评估表明，该框架能生成紧凑干预计划，并能通过语义验证发现纯优化方法无法识别的不可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习分析模型虽能识别学业风险学生，但无法直接指出哪些干预措施在实际教育场景中可行、可操作且符合约束条件。作者旨在弥合"模型预测"与"实际操作"之间的鸿沟，使反事实追索建议在教育环境中更具操作意义。

Method: SC2R框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 校准预测模型用于识别风险学生；(2) 基于整数规划的反事实追索生成，作用于离散动作变量；(3) 轻量级RDF词汇表用于干预计划表示；(4) SHACL验证机制用于强制执行时间、预算、不可变性和可用性约束。评估基于OULAD数据集，针对每次评估构建相对快照，在两个决策时间范围上进行离线实验。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 预测组件具有较强性能；(2) 能在规模化场景下生成紧凑的干预计划；(3) 语义验证能够揭示纯优化设置下会被接受的不可行计划，从而避免了不切实际建议的产出。

Conclusion: 本文不声称对学生结果有因果改善，而是论证了当反事实追索建议不仅通过模型验证，还通过语义可行性和机器可检查性验证时，其在教育中的操作意义将显著增强。SC2R为教育决策支持系统提供了一种从"只建模"到"可执行"的范式转变。

Abstract: Learning analytics models can identify students at risk of poor performance, but they do not directly indicate which interventions are feasible, actionable, and compatible with educational constraints. This paper introduces SC2R, a semantics-constrained counterfactual recourse framework for educational decision support. SC2R combines a calibrated predictive model, integer-programming-based recourse generation over discrete action variables, a lightweight RDF vocabulary for intervention-plan representation, and SHACL validation for enforcing timing, budget, immutability, and availability constraints. The framework is evaluated offline on the OULAD dataset using snapshots constructed relative to each assessment at two decision horizons. Results show that the predictive component provides strong performance, that compact intervention plans can be generated at scale, and that semantic validation reveals infeasible plans that lighter optimization-only settings would otherwise accept. Rather than claiming causal improvement in student outcomes, this work shows that counterfactual recourse becomes more operationally meaningful in education when recommendations are not only model-valid, but also semantically feasible and machine-checkable.

</details>


### [28] [DEPT: Document Embedding Preservation Tuning for Unified Query Expansion and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17632)
*Jingyuan Wang,Richong Zhang,Zhijie Nie,Mingxin Li,Yanzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DEPT方法，将大型语言模型统一用于查询扩展和文档编码，通过文档嵌入保持调优（DEPT）解决端到端训练中的移动目标问题，在BEIR基准五个数据集上优于各基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM检索系统要么依赖提示词扩展，要么使用独立训练模块或分阶段优化，导致生成的扩展与检索损失之间缺乏直接对齐。作者希望用一个统一的解码器模型同时完成查询扩展和检索编码，但端到端训练中检索监督同时更新查询扩展和文档嵌入，形成移动目标问题。

Method: DEPT通过文档嵌入保持调优，使微调后的文档嵌入接近缓存的初始嵌入，同时允许检索梯度通过直通式解码（straight-through decoding）传入生成器。它将联合的查询-文档变动转化为仅查询侧的适应，配合近似稳定的白化文档嵌入，支持索引复用和在线困难负样本挖掘。

Result: 使用Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507和LLaMA-3.2-3B-Instruct在BEIR基准的五个数据集上实验，DEPT在平均检索质量上优于无训练、独立训练和分阶段统一的基线方法；消融实验分别验证了保持调优、白化、端到端扩展训练和在线负样本的作用。

Conclusion: DEPT证明了统一的端到端查询扩展与检索模型是可行的，通过文档嵌入保持调优有效解决了移动目标问题，在不牺牲索引可用性的前提下提升了检索性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can both expand underspecified queries and encode text as dense representations, suggesting a unified model for query expansion and retrieval. Existing systems usually rely on prompted expansions, independently trained modules, or staged optimization, leaving generated expansions only indirectly aligned with the retrieval loss that judges them. We train a single decoder-only LLM end to end, where the same model generates the expansion and encodes both the expanded query and candidate documents. This unified setting creates a moving-target problem: retrieval supervision should improve query-side expansion, but the same update also shifts the document embeddings that serve as retrieval targets. We introduce Document Embedding Preservation Tuning (DEPT), which keeps tuned document embeddings close to cached initial embeddings while allowing retrieval gradients to pass through straight-through decoding into the generator. DEPT converts joint query--document movement into query-side adaptation against approximately stable, whitened document embeddings that support index reuse and online hard-negative mining. Experiments with Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 and LLaMA-3.2-3B-Instruct on five datasets in BEIR benchmark show that DEPT improves average retrieval quality over training-free, independently trained, and staged unified baselines, while ablations isolate the effects of preservation, whitening, end-to-end expansion training, and online negatives. Code is available at https://github.com/ILSparkle/DEPT.

</details>


### [29] [VisDocAgentBench: Benchmarking Agents for Visually Rich Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17889)
*Lexiang Hu,Yanzhao Zhang,Mingxin Li,Dingkun Long,Yikang Li,Fuwei Zhang,Yisen Wang,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VisDocAgentBench是一个封闭语料库基准，用于对比静态检索与智能体检索在视觉丰富文档上的排名表现，发现迭代式证据获取对多跳检索至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索评估通常采用一次性查询-页面匹配，而智能体搜索基准往往只评估下游QA或报告生成，文档排序在迭代证据获取下的表现仍然缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建了包含2,375页/100篇文档和120个查询的基准，查询按直接、一跳桥接、两跳桥接三种证据结构分类；采用关系保持构建方法生成语义、关系性和视觉查询，并经过全文审查和硬负样本验证。对比了late-interaction视觉检索器与多种智能体检索规划器的表现。

Result: 强视觉检索器在直接项上达97.50% Recall@1，但在两跳桥接项上仅2.50%；智能体检索可恢复大部分损失，视觉检索的最佳R@1为67.50%，而OCR文字仅37.50%。消融实验确认迭代搜索和页面检查是关键能力，提供完整支持上下文能改善两条路线的排名。

Conclusion: 现有静态检索在依赖语料上下文的多跳检索中严重退化，需要结合模态保持的发现能力与证据导向的验证能力的检索智能体。

Abstract: Visually rich documents encode relevance through language, layout, structured visual elements, and corpus context, yet retrieval is typically evaluated by one-shot query--page matching. Agentic-search benchmarks usually score downstream question answering or report generation, leaving document ranking under iterative evidence acquisition underexplored. We introduce VisDocAgentBench, a closed-corpus benchmark comparing static and agentic retrieval under a shared ranked-output contract. It contains 2,375 pages from 100 documents and 120 unique-target queries balanced across direct, one-bridge, and two-bridge evidence structures. Relation-preserving construction yields semantic, relational, and visual queries, followed by full-document review and hard-negative validation. A strong late-interaction visual retriever reaches 97.50% Recall@1 on direct items but 2.50% on two-bridge items, exposing the limits of query--target matching when relevance depends on corpus context. Agents recover much of this loss, but planner choice and retrieval representation remain decisive. Every planner performs better with visual retrieval, whose best R@1 reaches 67.50% versus 37.50% for OCR-text. Ablations identify iterative search and page inspection as consequential capabilities, and providing the complete support context improves ranking on both routes. Trace analysis localizes the remaining losses to target discovery, candidate examination, and evidence-role integration. These findings motivate retrieval agents that combine modality-preserving discovery with evidence-directed verification.

</details>


### [30] [Intent-Driven Dynamic Chunking: Segmenting Documents to Reflect Predicted Information Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14784)
*Christos Koutsiaris*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"意图驱动的动态分块"（IDC）的新方法，利用大语言模型预测用户意图，并通过动态规划算法找到全局最优的文档分块边界。在六个问答数据集上的实验表明，IDC相比传统分块策略在五个数据集上提升了5%-67%的top-1检索准确率，同时减少了40%-60%的分块数量并保持93%-100%的答案覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的文档分块方法（如固定长度分块或基于连贯性的分块）忽视了用户意图，导致答案被拆分或分块中包含无关噪声，进而影响信息检索系统（如搜索引擎、问答系统和RAG）的检索效果。因此，需要一种能够融入用户意图的智能分块方法。

Method: IDC方法包含两个核心步骤：首先利用大语言模型为文档生成可能的用户意图（即预测查询），然后采用动态规划算法在全局范围内寻找最优的分块边界，以避免贪心算法可能导致的次优解。这是动态规划在意图感知分块领域的新应用。

Result: 在六个多样的问答数据集（涵盖新闻、维基百科、学术论文和技术文档）上，IDC在五个数据集中超越了传统分块方法，top-1检索准确率提升了5%到67%；在第六个数据集中与最佳基线持平。此外，IDC生成的分块数量比基线方法少40%-60%，同时实现了93%-100%的答案覆盖率。

Conclusion: 将文档结构与人预期的信息需求对齐可以显著提升检索性能，尤其是对于长文档和异质文档。意图驱动的分块策略为信息检索中的文档分割问题提供了新的解决思路，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Breaking long documents into smaller segments is a fundamental challenge in information retrieval. Whether for search engines, question-answering systems, or retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), effective segmentation determines how well systems can locate and return relevant information. However, traditional methods, such as fixed-length or coherence-based segmentation, ignore user intent, leading to chunks that split answers or contain irrelevant noise. We introduce Intent-Driven Dynamic Chunking (IDC), a novel approach that uses predicted user queries to guide document segmentation. IDC leverages a Large Language Model to generate likely user intents for a document and then employs a dynamic programming algorithm to find the globally optimal chunk boundaries. This represents a novel application of DP to intent-aware segmentation that avoids greedy pitfalls. We evaluated IDC on six diverse question-answering datasets, including news articles, Wikipedia, academic papers, and technical documentation. IDC outperformed traditional chunking strategies on five datasets, improving top-1 retrieval accuracy by 5% to 67%, and matched the best baseline on the sixth. Additionally, IDC produced 40-60% fewer chunks than baseline methods while achieving 93-100% answer coverage. These results demonstrate that aligning document structure with anticipated information needs significantly boosts retrieval performance, particularly for long and heterogeneous documents.

</details>

### [31] [Brief Announcement: Fair Binding for Hidden-State Authorization in Byzantine SMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17349)
*Arnab Mallick*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究拜占庭容错状态机复制（BMR）中，当代理授权使命令的有效性依赖于隐藏策略状态时，安全分配隐藏可消耗资源所需的两个独立条件：公平排序约束到达顺序与提交顺序，以及首个请求必须绑定后续有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统验证式拜占庭SMR假设副本能评估命令的有效性，但代理授权场景下命令有效性仅相对于提交的策略状态成立，且副本无法从日志重建该状态，导致隐藏资源的后续分配存在安全风险。

Method: 提出单次预留（one-shot reservation）模型，通过显式授权见证接口分离两个义务：一是公平排序协议使到达顺序约束提交顺序，二是提交的首个请求绑定后续请求的有效性（而非仅记录历史授权），并设计公平的reserve/use协议。

Result: 证明了两个需求是独立且非空洞的，给出了同时满足授权安全性和首达活性（first-arrival liveness）的公平reserve/use协议，并指出在可信FIFO准入下两个需求合并。

Conclusion: 隐藏可消耗资源的安全实时分配在拜占庭领导者下需要同时满足公平排序和请求有效性绑定两个条件，二者在原子化场景中才可简化。

Abstract: Validated Byzantine SMR assumes that replicas can evaluate the validity of an ordered command. Agent authorization creates a different regime: a command may be valid only relative to a committed policy state that validators cannot reconstruct from the log. A proof that an action was authorized at an old commitment is then only a historical attestation, it does not by itself reserve the hidden resource for later use.
  We isolate two independent requirements for safe live allocation of a hidden consumable resource under a Byzantine leader. First, arrival order at correct replicas must constrain commit order, the gap addressed by fair-ordering protocols. Second, a committed first request must bind later validity: it must make conflicting later requests invalid, not merely record that the first request was once authorized. The second requirement is non-vacuous precisely because the current policy state is hidden and not prefix-recoverable. Using an explicit authorization-witness interface, we characterize the two distinct obligations in this one-shot reservation model and give a fair reserve/use protocol satisfying both authorization safety and first-arrival liveness. Under trusted FIFO admission the two requirements collapse because admission and execution are atomic, Byzantine SMR separates request commitment from use.

</details>
